//! Bind key operands once, retaining authored sequencing and contextual literals.
use super::*;

impl<'a> Binder<'a> {
    pub(super) fn bind_key_parts(&mut self, info: &TableInfo, read: &ReadExpr, end: usize,
        keys: &mut Vec<(Option<String>, Expr, Ty)>, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<()> {
        for (position, part) in read.keys.iter().enumerate().take(end).skip(keys.len()) {
            let mut setup = setup::Body::default();
            let expected = part.column.as_deref().or_else(|| read.index.as_ref().and_then(|name| info.indexes.iter().find(|i| &i.name == name)).and_then(|i| i.columns.get(position)).map(String::as_str)).and_then(|column| key_identity(info, column));
            let (e, t) = match expected {
                Some(expected) => self.expr_as_reference(&part.value, &mut setup, &expected)?,
                None => self.expr(&part.value, &mut setup)?,
            };
            self.sequence_setup(keys.iter_mut().map(|(_, e, _)| e), setup, out);
            let e = if expr::allocates(&e) { self.snapshot(e, out) } else { e };
            keys.push((part.column.clone(), e, t));
        }
        if let Some(index) = read.index.as_ref().and_then(|name| info.indexes.iter().find(|i| &i.name == name)) {
            for (position,(column,value,ty)) in keys.iter_mut().enumerate() {
                if let Some(expected) = column.as_deref().or_else(|| index.columns.get(position).map(String::as_str)).and_then(|c| key_identity(info,c)) {
                    (*value,*ty) = self.row_identity(value.clone(),ty.clone(),&expected);
                }
            }
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    // Mixed-key normalization names an already-bound positional prefix. Only
    // a literal acquires new context; compound expressions keep their IR/setup.
    pub(super) fn name_bound_keys(&mut self, info: &TableInfo, read: &ReadExpr,
        keys: &mut [(Option<String>, Expr, Ty)]) -> Fallible<()> {
        for ((column, expr, ty), part) in keys.iter_mut().zip(&read.keys) {
            *column = part.column.clone();
            if let Some(expected) = column.as_deref().and_then(|column| key_identity(info, column))
                && matches!(&part.value, A::Literal { value: Literal::Str(_), .. }) {
                (*expr, *ty) = self.expr_as_reference(&part.value, &mut setup::Body::default(), &expected)?;
            }
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

fn key_identity(info: &TableInfo, column: &str) -> Option<Ty> {
    let ty = if column == "id" { Ty::Ref(info.decl.name.clone()) }
        else { Ty::from_scalar(info.columns.get(column)?) };
    ty.is_identity().then_some(ty)
}
                self.job_bound_helpers.insert(self.call_chain.last().unwrap().0.clone());
            }
        }
        if self.caller_kind != ProgramKind::Job && (position != prefix::Position::Aggregate || self.caller_kind == ProgramKind::Query) { self.check_meter_prefix(read, &scan, position == prefix::Position::Aggregate)?; }
        if position != prefix::Position::Aggregate { self.shape_meter_note(&scan, read); }
        Ok((scan, range.take))
    }

    /// Evaluate the keys, then choose the index: explicit `by`, else by
    /// key names, count and types. Point reads need a unique full key;
    /// ranges need a strict prefix.
    fn select_index(&mut self, info: &'a TableInfo, read: &ReadExpr, mode: ReadMode, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Vec<(Option<String>, Expr, Ty)>, ast::IndexDecl, String)> {
        // @ref LLP 3000.013#2.2 — retain bound keys for type selection and final lowering.
        // No cloned expression pass: nested lookups must not multiply binder work.
        let mut keys = Vec::<(Option<String>, Expr, Ty)>::new();
        let mixed = read.keys.iter().any(|key| key.column.is_some()) && read.keys.iter().any(|key| key.column.is_none());
        let types = if mixed {
            let prefix = read.keys.iter().take_while(|key| key.column.is_none()).count();
            self.bind_key_parts(info, read, prefix, &mut keys, out)?;
            Some(keys.iter().map(|(_, expr, ty)| (ty.clone(), matches!(expr, Expr::Literal(Value::String(_))))).collect::<Vec<_>>())
        } else { None };
        let mut normalized = super::mixed_keys::read_typed(info, read, self.catalog, types.as_deref())?;
        self.name_bound_keys(info, &normalized, &mut keys)?;
        let read = normalized.as_ref();
        super::validate_key_names(read)?;
        let mut positional_reason = None;
        if !read.keys.is_empty() && read.keys.iter().all(|key| key.column.is_none()) {
            let declared = |index: &&ast::IndexDecl| !self.catalog.synthesized.contains(&(read.table.clone(), index.name.clone()));
            let mut candidates = info.indexes.iter().filter(declared).filter(|index| match &read.index {
                Some(name) => &index.name == name,
                None => index.unique && index.columns.len() == read.keys.len(),
            }).collect::<Vec<_>>();
            if read.index.is_none() && candidates.len() > 1 {
                self.bind_key_parts(info, read, read.keys.len(), &mut keys, out)?;
                let types = keys.iter().map(|(_, expr, ty)| (ty.clone(), matches!(expr, Expr::Literal(Value::String(_))))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
                candidates = super::positional_keys::candidates(info, read, &types, &self.catalog.synthesized);
            }
            if read.index.is_none() && mode == ReadMode::Range && read.order_keys.is_empty() && candidates.len() == 1 {
                let scan = info.indexes.iter().filter(declared).find(|index| !index.unique && index.is_plain() && index.columns.len() > read.keys.len());
                let columns = scan.map(|index| index.columns.as_slice()).unwrap_or(&candidates[0].columns);
                let predicate = read.keys.iter().zip(columns).map(|(key, column)| format!(".{column} = {}", self.catalog.source.operand(&key.value))).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" and ");
                return Err(Diagnostic::new(&read.site, "E_QUARRY_POSITIONAL_SCAN", "a positional key selects one declared unique row, not a scan prefix",
                    format!("{} where {predicate} first 20 by .id", read.table)).guide("reads"));
            }
            if candidates.len() != 1 {
                return Err(super::positional_keys::refusal(info, read, &candidates, self.catalog));
            }
            if read.index.is_none() {
                positional_reason = Some(if info.indexes.iter().filter(declared).filter(|index| index.unique && index.columns.len() == read.keys.len()).count() == 1 {
                    "only declared unique key of this arity"
                } else { "unique positional key selected by argument types" });
                normalized.to_mut().index = Some(candidates[0].name.clone());
            }
        }
        let read = normalized.as_ref();
        let point = mode != ReadMode::Range;
        self.bind_key_parts(info, read, read.keys.len(), &mut keys, out)?;
        let named = keys.iter().all(|(c, _, _)| c.is_some()) && !keys.is_empty();
        let mut selection = if read.index.is_some() { "explicit by" } else if named { "named equality columns" } else { "compatible positional key types" };
        // A `words(..)` index serves one read: `matching` with exactly its
        // leading columns as keys. Every other read sees plain indexes only.
        let searching = mode == ReadMode::Range && read.matching.is_some();
        let mut candidates: Vec<&ast::IndexDecl> = info
            .indexes
            .iter()
            .filter(|index| read.index.as_ref().is_none_or(|explicit| explicit == &index.name))
            .filter(|index| read.seeks.iter().all(|b| index.columns.get(keys.len()) == Some(&b.column)))
            .filter(|index| if searching { index.searches_after(keys.len()) } else { index.words.is_none() })
            .filter(|index| match mode {
                ReadMode::Point | ReadMode::FilteredPoint => index.unique && (index.columns.len() == keys.len() || (named && mode == ReadMode::FilteredPoint && info.total_unique(index) && index.columns.len() < keys.len())),
                ReadMode::Exists => (index.columns.len() == keys.len() && (named || read.index.is_some() || index.unique)) || (named && info.total_unique(index) && index.columns.len() < keys.len()),
                ReadMode::Range => index.columns.len() > keys.len() || (!read.order_keys.is_empty() && index.unique && index.columns.len() == keys.len()),
            })
            .filter(|index| {
                if named {
                    if index.columns.len() < keys.len() { index.columns.iter().all(|column| keys.iter().any(|(c, _, _)| c.as_ref() == Some(column))) } else { keys.iter().all(|(c, _, _)| index.columns[..keys.len()].contains(c.as_ref().unwrap())) }
                } else {
                    true
                }
            })
            .collect();
        if named && matches!(mode, ReadMode::FilteredPoint | ReadMode::Exists) {
            if let Some(width) = candidates.iter().filter(|i| info.total_unique(i)).map(|i| i.columns.len()).min() {
                candidates.retain(|i| info.total_unique(i) && i.columns.len() == width);
                // @ref LLP 3000.036#15.7 — named equalities covering two total unique keys
                // (`loans[borrower = viewer, telescope = t]`, lane 057) test one row either
                // way, the other key's columns being extra equalities on it; the first
                // declared key decides, as a rule probe already does (§11).
                // Same-column keys in two orders (`unique pair: low, high` and `unique
                // inverse: high, low`) still need an explicit `by` (guide indexes).
                let mut sets = candidates.iter().map(|index| { let mut columns = index.columns.clone(); columns.sort(); columns }).collect::<Vec<_>>();
                sets.dedup();
                if candidates.len() > 1 && sets.len() == candidates.len() {
                    selection = "first declared unique key the named equalities cover";
                    candidates.truncate(1);
                }
            } else if candidates.iter().any(|i| i.unique) {
                // Exact nullable unique keys still select points. A competing
                // nonunique index must not turn an exists probe into a tie.
                candidates.retain(|i| i.unique);
            }
        }
        // Positional keys select by type; a text literal may meet an
        // identity column (it becomes an id once the index is chosen).
        if !named {
            candidates.retain(|index| {
                keys.iter().enumerate().all(|(position, (_, e, t))| {
                    index.columns.get(position).is_some_and(|column| {
                        let expected = self.key_ty(info, index, column);
                        t.comparable(&expected) || (expected.is_identity() && matches!(e, Expr::Literal(Value::String(_))))
                    })
                })
            });
            // @ref LLP 3000.007#18.5 — resolve remaining ambiguity without replacing an existing exact-type winner.
            let exact: Vec<_> = candidates.iter().copied().filter(|index| keys.iter().enumerate().all(|(position, (_, _, t))| index.columns.get(position).is_some_and(|column| self.key_ty(info, index, column) == t.required()))).collect();
            if exact.len() == 1 {
                candidates = exact;
                if read.index.is_none() { selection = "single exact positional type match"; }
            }
            else {
                // Filter before the one-candidate arm so identity literals receive their index's type.
                let unique: Vec<_> = candidates.iter().copied().filter(|index| index.unique && index.columns.len() == keys.len()).collect();
                if unique.len() == 1 {
                    candidates = unique;
                    if read.index.is_none() { selection = "only compatible full unique key"; }
                }
            }
        }
        if let Some(explicit) = &read.index
            && candidates.is_empty()
        {
            let known = info.indexes.iter().find(|i| &i.name == explicit);
            return Err(match known {
                None => unknown(&read.site, format!("{} has no index {explicit}", read.table), name_rewrite(explicit, info.indexes.iter().map(|i| i.name.as_str()), None)),
                Some(index) if index.words.is_some() && !searching => unknown(&read.site, format!("{}.{explicit} is a words index; it is read with matching", read.table), format!("{}[{}] matching q first 50 by {explicit}", read.table, index.columns[..index.words.as_ref().map_or(0, |(p, _)| *p)].join(", "))),
                Some(index) if searching && index.words.is_none() => self.matching_nonwords_index(info, read, index),
                Some(index) if searching => Diagnostic::new(&read.site, "E_QUARRY_MATCHING_INDEX", format!("{}.{explicit} has its words after {} column{}; matching takes exactly those as keys", read.table, index.words.as_ref().map_or(0, |(p, _)| *p), if index.words.as_ref().map_or(0, |(p, _)| *p) == 1 { "" } else { "s" }), format!("{}[{}] matching q first 50 by {explicit}", read.table, index.columns[..index.words.as_ref().map_or(0, |(p, _)| *p)].join(", "))),
                Some(index) => self.index_mismatch(info, read, mode, &keys, index),
            });
        }
        match candidates.len() {
            1 => {
                let index = candidates[0].clone();
                if mode == ReadMode::Range && read.index.is_none() && !read.keys.is_empty() && !named {
                    let predicate = read.keys.iter().zip(&index.columns).map(|(key, column)| format!(".{column} = {}", self.catalog.source.operand(&key.value))).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" and ");
                    let order = index.columns.iter().skip(read.keys.len()).map(|column| format!(".{column}")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");
                    return Err(Diagnostic::new(&read.site, "E_QUARRY_POSITIONAL_SCAN", format!("{} would select non-unique prefix {}", print::read_expr(read), index.name), format!("{} where {predicate} first 50 by {order}", read.table)).guide("reads"));
                }
                if !named {
                    for (position, (_, e, t)) in keys.iter_mut().enumerate() {
                        if let Some(column) = index.columns.get(position)
                            && self.key_ty(info, &index, column).is_identity()
                            && let Expr::Literal(Value::String(text)) = e
                        {
                            *e = Expr::Literal(Value::Id(text.clone()));
                            *t = Ty::Id;
                        }
                    }
                }
                Ok((keys, index, positional_reason.unwrap_or(selection).into()))
            }
            0 => {
                let declared: Vec<String> = info.indexes.iter().map(|i| format!("{} ({})", i.name, i.columns.join(", "))).collect();
                if searching {
                    let scope: Vec<String> = keys.iter().map(|(c, _, _)| c.clone().unwrap_or_else(|| "<column>".into())).collect();
                    Err(unknown(&read.site, format!("{} has no words index after {} key{}; its indexes are {}", read.table, keys.len(), if keys.len() == 1 { "" } else { "s" }, declared.join(", ")), format!("by by{}Words: {}words(<text column>), at, id", scope.iter().map(|c| title(c)).collect::<String>(), scope.iter().map(|c| format!("{c}, ")).collect::<String>())))
                } else if point {
                    Err(unknown(&read.site, format!("{} has no unique index over {} column{} matching this key; its indexes are {}", read.table, keys.len(), if keys.len() == 1 { "" } else { "s" }, declared.join(", ")), format!("unique by{}: {}", keys.iter().map(|(c, _, _)| c.clone().unwrap_or_else(|| "Column".into())).map(|c| title(&c)).collect::<String>(), keys.iter().map(|(c, _, _)| c.clone().unwrap_or_else(|| "<column>".into())).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ")) + if named && mode != ReadMode::Point { "  -- an index is needed when the named equalities do not cover a total unique key" } else { "" }))
                } else {
                    Err(unknown(&read.site, format!("{} has no index whose prefix takes {} key{}; its indexes are {}", read.table, keys.len(), if keys.len() == 1 { "" } else { "s" }, declared.join(", ")), format!("by by{}: {}, id", keys.iter().map(|(c, _, _)| c.clone().unwrap_or_else(|| "Column".into())).map(|c| title(&c)).collect::<String>(), keys.iter().map(|(c, _, _)| c.clone().unwrap_or_else(|| "<column>".into())).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", "))).guide("indexes"))
                }
            }
            _ => {
                let names: Vec<&str> = candidates.iter().map(|i| i.name.as_str()).collect();
                let changes = candidates.iter().filter(|index| !self.catalog.synthesized.contains(&(read.table.clone(), index.name.clone())))
                    .map(|index| crate::repair::ReadChange::Index(index.name.clone())).collect();
                Err(ambiguous(&read.site, format!("{} has {} indexes matching this key: {}; choose an index valid for this read and its arguments", read.table, names.len(), names.join(", ")), "")
                    .repair_read(&read.site, changes))
            }
        }
    }

    fn key_ty(&self, info: &TableInfo, index: &ast::IndexDecl, column: &str) -> Ty {
        if index.computed.contains_key(column) { return Ty::Int; }
        if column == "id" {
            return Ty::Ref(info.decl.name.clone());
        }
        info.columns.get(column).map(Ty::from_scalar).unwrap_or(Ty::Unknown)
    }

    fn order_keys(&mut self, info: &TableInfo, index: &ast::IndexDecl, keys: Vec<(Option<String>, Expr, Ty)>) -> Fallible<Vec<Expr>> {
        let named = keys.iter().all(|(c, _, _)| c.is_some());
        if !named {
            for (position, (_, _, t)) in keys.iter().enumerate() {
                let expected = self.key_ty(info, index, &index.columns[position]);
                if !t.comparable(&expected) {
                    return Err(type_error(&index.site, format!("key {} is {}; {}.{}.{} is {}", position + 1, t.name(), info.decl.name, index.name, index.columns[position], expected.name()), format!("use {} for key {}", expected.name(), position + 1)));
                }
            }
            return Ok(keys.into_iter().map(|(_, e, _)| e).collect());
        }
        let mut ordered = Vec::new();
        for column in index.columns.iter().take(keys.len()) {
            let Some(position) = keys.iter().position(|(c, _, _)| c.as_deref() == Some(column)) else {
                return Err(unknown(&index.site, format!("key omits column {column} of {}.{}", info.decl.name, index.name), format!("name each prefix column once: {}", index.columns.iter().take(keys.len()).cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", "))));
            };
            let expected = self.key_ty(info, index, column);
            if !keys[position].2.comparable(&expected) {
                return Err(type_error(&index.site, format!("{column} is {}; {}.{column} is {}", keys[position].2.name(), info.decl.name, expected.name()), format!("use {} for {column}", expected.name())));
            }
            ordered.push(keys[position].1.clone());
        }
        Ok(ordered)
    }

    fn walk(&mut self, table: &str, seed: &A, column: &str, depth: u32, site: &Site, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Recurse, Ty)> {
            Self::Read => "read",
            Self::Insert => "insert",
            Self::Update => "update",
            Self::Delete => "delete",
        })
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum RuleExpr {
    Public { reason: String },
    Allow,
    Deny,
    Eq(RuleTerm, RuleTerm),
    Ne(RuleTerm, RuleTerm),
    /// Ordered comparisons are confined to named update transitions and to
    /// insert rules over the row's own required int/time columns.
    Compare(crate::ir::BinaryOp, RuleTerm, RuleTerm),
    And(Vec<RuleExpr>),
    Or(Vec<RuleExpr>),
    Not(Box<RuleExpr>),
    Exists {
        table: String, index: String, key: Vec<RuleTerm>, provenance: KeyProvenance,
        #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Vec::is_empty")]
        equalities: Vec<(String, RuleTerm)>,
    },
    /// True only when the indexed target row was inserted earlier in this
    /// same transaction; insert rules only.
    Created {
        table: String, index: String, key: Vec<RuleTerm>, provenance: KeyProvenance,
        #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Vec::is_empty")]
        equalities: Vec<(String, RuleTerm)>,
    },
}

impl RuleExpr {
    pub fn is_public(&self) -> bool {
        matches!(self, Self::Public { .. })
    }

    pub fn is_uniformly_readable(&self) -> bool {
        matches!(self, Self::Public { .. } | Self::Allow)
    }

    /// Every `.field` the rule reads, on any probe key or comparison.
    pub fn fields(&self, out: &mut BTreeSet<String>) {
        let term = |term: &RuleTerm, out: &mut BTreeSet<String>| {
            if let RuleTerm::Field(field) | RuleTerm::Reference { column: field, .. } | RuleTerm::ReferenceChain { column: field, .. } = term {
                out.insert(field.clone());
            }
        };
        match self {
            Self::Public { .. } | Self::Allow | Self::Deny => {}
            Self::Eq(left, right) | Self::Ne(left, right) | Self::Compare(_, left, right) => {
                term(left, out);
                term(right, out);
            }
            Self::And(items) | Self::Or(items) => items.iter().for_each(|item| item.fields(out)),
            Self::Not(inner) => inner.fields(out),
            Self::Exists { key, equalities, .. } | Self::Created { key, equalities, .. } => {
                key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_, term)| term)).for_each(|item| term(item, out));
            }
        }
    }

    /// The disjuncts of the rule: an `or` flattens, anything else is itself.
    pub fn disjuncts(&self) -> Vec<&RuleExpr> {
        match self {
            Self::Or(items) => items.iter().flat_map(|item| item.disjuncts()).collect(),
            other => vec![other],
        }
    }

    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.6 — service seats read on the server and never enlarge a device's audience.
    pub fn service_grant(&self) -> Option<&str> {
        match self {
            Self::Eq(RuleTerm::Viewer, RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Id(principal)))
            | Self::Eq(RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Id(principal)), RuleTerm::Viewer)
                if principal.strip_prefix("service:").is_some_and(|name| !name.is_empty()) => Some(principal),
            _ => None,
        }
    }

    /// Remove only entire service disjuncts; a conjunct, negation or ordinary
    /// principal equality must still pass the ordinary exact-audience check.
    pub fn device_audience_rule(&self) -> std::borrow::Cow<'_, RuleExpr> {
        let terms = self.disjuncts();
        if !terms.iter().any(|term| term.service_grant().is_some()) { return std::borrow::Cow::Borrowed(self); }
        let mut audience: Vec<_> = terms.into_iter().filter(|term| term.service_grant().is_none()).cloned().collect();
        std::borrow::Cow::Owned(match audience.len() {
            0 => Self::Deny,
//! Developer-owned, in-memory refusal context. Never part of Refusal or receipts.
// @ref LLP 3000.015#2.1 — only a host opt-in can collect the private refusing clause.
use super::runner::Runner;
use crate::{RuntimeError, schema::Effect};

#[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
#[derive(Clone, Debug, serde::Serialize)]
pub struct RefusingClause {
    pub table: String,
    pub viewer: String,
    pub clause: String,
    pub phase: String,
    pub terminal: bool,
    pub no_candidate: bool,
    pub site: String,
    pub generation: u64,
}
#[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default)]
pub struct RefusalTrace(std::sync::Arc<std::sync::Mutex<Option<RefusingClause>>>, std::sync::Arc<std::sync::atomic::AtomicU64>);
#[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
impl RefusalTrace {
    /// Physical row images encountered and filtered by read authority in this request.
    pub fn hidden(&self) -> u64 { self.1.load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed) }
    pub fn take(&self) -> Option<RefusingClause> { self.0.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner()).take() }
}
impl Runner<'_> {
    // @ref LLP 3000.031#1.2 — only a real, uncapped storage miss; never replace a clause.
    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
    pub(super) fn no_candidate(&self, table: &str, site: &str) {
        if let Some(trace) = &self.context.refusal_trace {
            trace.0.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner()).get_or_insert_with(|| RefusingClause {
                table: table.into(), viewer: self.context.viewer.clone(), clause: "no-candidate".into(), phase: "storage probe".into(),
                terminal: false, no_candidate: true, site: site.into(), generation: self.context.generation,
            });
        }
    }
    pub(super) fn refusing_clause(&self, table: &str, clause: &str) {
        self.refusing_phase(table, clause, clause);
    }
    pub(super) fn filtered_clause(&self, table: &str, clause: &str) {
        self.filtered_phase(table, clause, clause);
    }
    pub(super) fn filtered_phase(&self, table: &str, clause: &str, phase: &str) {
        self.record_clause(table, clause, phase, false);
    }
    pub(super) fn refusing_phase(&self, table: &str, clause: &str, phase: &str) {
        self.record_clause(table, clause, phase, true);
    }
    fn record_clause(&self, table: &str, clause: &str, phase: &str, terminal: bool) {
        #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
        if let Some(trace) = &self.context.refusal_trace {
            *trace.0.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner()) = Some(RefusingClause {
                table: table.into(), viewer: self.context.viewer.clone(), clause: clause.into(), phase: phase.into(), terminal, no_candidate: false, site: self.statement_site.clone(), generation: self.context.generation,
            });
        }
        #[cfg(not(feature = "sqlite"))]
        let _ = (table, clause, phase, terminal);
    }
    pub(super) fn trace_rule_result(&self, result: &Result<bool, RuntimeError>, table: &str, effect: Effect) {
        #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
        if self.context.refusal_trace.is_some() {
            let phase = format!("{effect} rule");
            if matches!(result, Ok(false)) { self.record_clause(table, &phase, &phase, effect != Effect::Read); }
            self.trace_authorization(result, table, effect, &phase);
        }
        #[cfg(not(feature = "sqlite"))]
        let _ = (result, table, effect);
    }
    /// Preserve the phase before enclosing writes remap the public error effect.
    pub(super) fn trace_authorization<T>(&self, result: &Result<T, RuntimeError>, table: &str, effect: Effect, phase: &str) {
        #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
        if self.context.refusal_trace.is_some() && matches!(result, Err(RuntimeError::AuthorizationBudget { .. })) {
            self.refusing_phase(table, &format!("authorization allowance ({effect})"), phase);
        }
        #[cfg(not(feature = "sqlite"))]
        let _ = (result, table, effect, phase);
    }
    pub(super) fn trace_unlocated_budget(&self, table: &str, effect: Effect) {
        #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
        if self.context.refusal_trace.as_ref().is_some_and(|trace| trace.0.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e|e.into_inner()).as_ref().is_none_or(|cause| !cause.clause.starts_with("authorization allowance"))) {
            self.refusing_phase(table, &format!("authorization allowance ({effect})"), "authorization access");
        }
        #[cfg(not(feature = "sqlite"))]
        let _ = (table, effect);
    }
    pub(super) fn hidden_row(&self) {
        #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
        if let Some(trace) = &self.context.refusal_trace { trace.1.fetch_add(1, std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed); }
    }
    pub(super) fn rule_refusal(&self, table: &str, effect: Effect, clause: &str) -> RuntimeError {
        self.refusing_clause(table, clause);
        RuntimeError::Rule { table: table.into(), effect }
    }
}
//! A row-read verdict and field projection together authorize result pricing.
use super::runner::Runner;
use crate::{error::{MeterKind, RuntimeError}, ir::Read, meter::Meter,
    schema::{Effect, Index}, value::{Disclosed, Value}};

// Only successful row authorization in this module can mint this evidence.
pub(crate) struct RowAuthorization(());
pub(crate) struct AuthorizedRow<'a> {
    projection: Disclosed<'a>,
    authority: RowAuthorization,
    // @ref LLP 3000.014#5 — a row's witness deadline is provisional until an answer emits it.
    expiry: super::expiry::Pending,
}
impl AuthorizedRow<'_> {
    pub(super) fn expiry(&self) -> super::expiry::Pending { self.expiry }
    pub(super) fn scalar_heap(&self, field: &str) -> u64 { self.projection.scalar_heap(field, &self.authority) }
    pub(crate) fn heap_bytes(&self) -> u64 { self.projection.heap_bytes(&self.authority) }
    pub(super) fn projection_heap(&self) -> u64 { self.projection.projection_heap(&self.authority) }
    pub(crate) fn materialization_heap(&self) -> u64 { self.projection.materialization_heap(&self.authority) }
    pub(super) fn into_value(self) -> Value { self.projection.into_value() }
    pub(super) fn into_parts(self) -> (Value, RowAuthorization) { (self.projection.into_value(), self.authority) }
    pub(super) fn charge(self, meter: &mut Meter) -> Result<Value, RuntimeError> {
        meter.heap(self.heap_bytes() + self.projection_heap() + self.materialization_heap())?;
        Ok(self.into_value())
    }
}

impl RowAuthorization {
    pub(super) fn project(self, projection: Disclosed<'_>) -> AuthorizedRow<'_> { AuthorizedRow { projection, authority:self, expiry: Default::default() } }
}

impl<'r> Runner<'r> {
    // @ref LLP 3000.015#1 — member reads share the row-read and field-disclosure capability.
    pub(super) fn authorized_member(&mut self, name: &str, id: &str, #[cfg(feature = "server-search")] trusted: bool) -> Result<Option<Value>, RuntimeError> {
        let table = &self.schema.tables[name];
        // @ref LLP 3000.024#decision — this id came from an authorized head in
        // this read-only transaction. Price its public image before decoding,
        // then recheck the rule with a separate witness reservation. Neither
        // hidden/expired images nor private fields can select actual-size admission.
        #[cfg(feature = "server-search")]
        if trusted && !self.device && !table.read_lifetime_sensitive() && table.recorded.is_empty()
            && table.through.is_none() && table.written_by.values().all(|a|a.audience) {
            if !self.read_charge(MeterKind::Examined,1)? { return Ok(None); }
            self.meter.heap(crate::rows::word_heap::text(id) + crate::rows::word_heap::slots::<Value>(1))?;
            let key = [Value::Id(id.into())];
            let Some((Some(row),_)) = self.scan_authorized(name,crate::schema::BY_ID,&key,&Default::default(),crate::rows::Dir::Asc,1,true,true,None)?.pop() else { return Ok(None); };
            let materialization = row.materialization_heap();
            let prepay = self.meter.mark();
            self.meter.heap(materialization)?;
            let expiry = self.next_expiry.scope();
            let row = self.authorized_scratch(self.rule_scratch(&table.rules.read), &[(MeterKind::RuleProbes,super::rules::rule_probes(&table.rules.read),
                )], |runner,_| {
                let row = runner.authorize_image(name,row.into_value())?;
                if let Some(row) = &row { runner.next_expiry.inherit(row.expiry()); }
                Ok(row)
            });
            // The value now owns the default. Transfer its prepay to charge(),
            // keeping only one retained reservation after the recheck.
            self.meter.release_row_heap(prepay, materialization);
            let row = row?;
            if row.is_some() { expiry.emit(); }
            return row.map(|row|row.charge(&mut self.meter)).transpose();
        }
        let bytes = crate::rows::disclosed::decode_reservation(table).saturating_add(self.rule_scratch(&table.rules.read));
        let expiry = self.next_expiry.scope();
        let row = self.authorized_scratch(bytes, &[(MeterKind::Examined, 1), (MeterKind::RuleProbes, super::rules::rule_probes(&table.rules.read),
                )], |runner, scope| {
            let Some(mut row) = runner.tx.get(scope, name, id)? else { return Ok(None); };
            crate::schema::materialize_optional_fields(&mut row, table);
            let until = table.deadline(&row);
            let authorized = runner.authorize_image(name, row)?;
            if let Some(row) = &authorized { runner.next_expiry.inherit(row.expiry()); runner.next_expiry.note_option(until); }
            Ok(authorized)
        })?;
        if row.is_some() { expiry.emit(); }
        row.map(|row|row.charge(&mut self.meter)).transpose()
    }

    pub(super) fn authorize_image(&mut self, name: &str, row: Value) -> Result<Option<AuthorizedRow<'r>>, RuntimeError> {
        let table = &self.schema.tables[name];
        // @ref LLP 3000.014#5-what-derives-from-the-capture — a denied or expired candidate's witness deadlines are discarded with its scope.
        let expiry = self.next_expiry.scope();
        if self.inactive(name, &row)? { return Ok(None); }
        if !self.check_rule(name, Effect::Read, None, &row)? {
            self.hidden_row();
            return Ok(None);
        }
        // Memory deadlines are hidden by Disclosed itself; transfer the live
        // boundary before projecting, still inside this candidate's expiry scope.
        if table.ephemeral.is_some() { self.note_expiry(table, &row); }
        let projection = self.disclosed_row(table, row).for_query();
        let visible = projection.into_value();
        Ok(Some(AuthorizedRow { projection: self.disclosed_row(table, visible).for_query(), authority: RowAuthorization(()), expiry: expiry.defer() }))
    }

    pub(super) fn scan_disclosed(&mut self, name: &str, index: &str, prefix: &[Value], bounds: &crate::rows::Bounds, dir: crate::rows::Dir, limit: usize) -> Result<Vec<(Option<AuthorizedRow<'r>>, Vec<u8>)>, RuntimeError> {
        self.scan_authorized(name,index,prefix,bounds,dir,limit,true,true,#[cfg(feature = "server-search")] None)
    }

    pub(super) fn scan_authorized(&mut self, name: &str, index: &str, prefix: &[Value], bounds: &crate::rows::Bounds, dir: crate::rows::Dir, limit: usize, metered: bool, trusted: bool, #[cfg(feature = "server-search")] fields: Option<&[String]>) -> Result<Vec<(Option<AuthorizedRow<'r>>, Vec<u8>)>, RuntimeError> {
        self.scan_authorized_prepaid(name, index, prefix, bounds, dir, limit, metered, trusted, false, #[cfg(feature = "server-search")] fields)
    }

    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.24.7 — a delivered uniform prefix has prepaid its
    // logical rule once; provisional rows still undergo physical authorization.
    pub(super) fn scan_authorized_prepaid(&mut self, name: &str, index: &str, prefix: &[Value], bounds: &crate::rows::Bounds, dir: crate::rows::Dir, limit: usize, metered: bool, trusted: bool, rule_prepaid: bool, #[cfg(feature = "server-search")] fields: Option<&[String]>) -> Result<Vec<(Option<AuthorizedRow<'r>>, Vec<u8>)>, RuntimeError> {
        let table = &self.schema.tables[name];
        // A public, non-expiring image is authorized before accessing storage.
        // No hidden candidate can influence its actual-size decoder admission.
        // Trusted selected heads may use the same actual-size adapter. It
        // decodes a full transient image; the streaming consumer retains only
        // its ordering key. Selection remains useful in the private decoder.
        if (self.context.is_system() || trusted && !table.read_lifetime_sensitive()) && table.recorded.is_empty() && table.through.is_none() && table.written_by.values().all(|a| a.audience) {
            if metered { self.meter.heap(crate::rows::word_heap::slots::<(Option<AuthorizedRow<'_>>,Vec<u8>,
                )>(limit))?; }
            #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
            let live = table.read_lifetime_sensitive();
            // A web device never reads as the system, the only reader of an expiring table here.
            #[cfg(not(feature = "sqlite"))]
            let live = false;
            let rows = if live { self.live_store_scan(name, index, prefix, bounds, dir, limit, metered)? }
            else if metered {
                self.metered_scan(|runner, scope| {
                    runner.tx.scan_metered(scope,name,index,prefix,bounds,dir,limit,&mut runner.meter)
                })?
            } else { self.metered_scan(|runner, scope| {
                    runner.tx.scan(scope,name,index,prefix,bounds,dir,limit)
                })? };
            // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.24.5 — a device prices the server's image with the server's charge. Its
            // own prediction rows still meet a non-uniform rule, as prepaid internal work outside the caller's
            // heap; after an earlier cut such a row stays hidden (the read is incomplete either way).
            let check = self.device && !self.context.is_system() && !table.rules.read.is_uniformly_readable();
            let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(rows.len());
            for (row, key) in rows {
                let row = if check && self.is_provisional(name, &row)? {
                    self.prepaid_scan(&[], false, |runner, scope| {
                        runner.candidate_verdict(scope, name, index, prefix, row, true, rule_prepaid)
                    })?
                } else { Some(AuthorizedRow { projection:self.disclosed_row(table,row),authority:RowAuthorization(()),expiry:Default::default() }) };
                out.push((row, key));
            }
            return Ok(out);
        }
        let bytes = crate::rows::disclosed::decode_reservation(table).saturating_add(self.rule_scratch(&table.rules.read));
        if metered { self.meter.heap(crate::rows::word_heap::slots::<(Option<AuthorizedRow<'_>>, Vec<u8>,
            )>(limit))?; }
        let mut cursor = bounds.clone();
        let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(limit);
        for _ in 0..limit {
            let candidate = self.reserved_scratch(bytes, &[], !trusted, |runner, scope| {
                loop {
                #[cfg(not(feature = "server-search"))]
                let selected = runner.tx.scan(scope,name,index,prefix,&cursor,dir,1)?;
                #[cfg(feature = "server-search")]
                let selected = if let Some(fields) = fields { runner.tx.scan_selected(scope,name,index,prefix,&cursor,dir,fields)? } else { runner.tx.scan(scope,name,index,prefix,&cursor,dir,1)? };
                let Some((row,key)) = selected.into_iter().next() else { return Ok(None); };
                // Advancing over a rejected candidate is internal decoder work.
                cursor.after = crate::key::decode(&key).map(|v|v.into_iter().skip(prefix.len()).collect());
                // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.24.6 — an expired, unswept candidate is never returned, counted or charged.
                if runner.step_inactive(name, &row, &key)? { continue; }
                return Ok(Some((runner.candidate_verdict(scope, name, index, prefix, row, trusted, rule_prepaid)?,key,
                    )));
                }
            })?;
            let Some((row,key)) = candidate else { break; };
            if let Some(row) = row {
                self.meter.heap(row.heap_bytes() + row.projection_heap() + row.materialization_heap()
                    + crate::rows::word_heap::key_decode(table.index(index).map_or(1, |i| i.components.len() + 1), key.len()))?;
            }
        }
        Ok(rows)
    }

    // @ref LLP 3000.015#1 — row and field authorization precede result pricing.
    pub(super) fn authorized_point(&mut self, read: &Read, index: &Index, key: &[Value], prepaid: bool, trusted: bool, prepaid_examined: bool, absent: &mut bool) -> Result<Option<AuthorizedRow<'r>>, RuntimeError> {
        let table = &self.schema.tables[&read.table];
        let row = if crate::rows::disclosed::actual_size(table) {
            if !prepaid_examined && !self.read_charge(MeterKind::Examined,1)? { self.point_cut(read, key); return Ok(None); }
            let scratch = self.meter.mark();
            let row = self.metered_scan(|runner, scope| {
                    runner.tx.scan_metered(scope,&read.table,&read.index,key,&Default::default(),crate::rows::Dir::Asc,1,&mut runner.meter)
                })?.pop();
            self.meter.release_heap(scratch);
            let Some((mut row,_)) = row else {
                *absent = true;
                self.point_miss(read, key);
                #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
                if !self.device && !self.read_capped() { self.no_candidate(&read.table, &read.site); }
                return Ok(None);
            };
            if !self.point_event_held(read, key, &row) {
                return Ok(None);
            }
            if self.device && self.retained_row(&read.table,&row)? { self.components.note(|frame|frame.retained=true); }
            crate::schema::materialize_optional_fields(&mut row,table);
            row
        } else {
            let bytes = crate::rows::disclosed::decode_reservation(table).saturating_add(self.rule_scratch(&table.rules.read));
            // @ref LLP 3000.014#5-what-derives-from-the-capture — a hidden, expired or absent candidate's deadlines are discarded with this scope.
            let expiry = self.next_expiry.scope();
            let authorized = self.authorized_scratch(bytes, &[(MeterKind::Examined, u64::from(!prepaid_examined))], |runner, scope| {
                #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
                let mut encountered = false;
                let Some(row) = runner.live_lookup(scope,&read.table, &read.index, index, key, #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")] Some(&mut encountered))? else {
                    *absent = true;
                    runner.point_miss(read, key);
                    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
                    if !encountered && !runner.device && !runner.read_capped() { runner.no_candidate(&read.table, &read.site); }
                    return Ok(None);
                };
                // @ref LLP 3000.016#21a-owned-events-follow-their-source-rows — a held source whose event kind is not fully synced answers nothing.
                if !runner.point_event_held(read, key, &row) {
                    return Ok(None);
                }
                if runner.inactive(&read.table, &row)? { return Ok(None); }
                let provisional = runner.is_provisional(&read.table, &row)?;
                let allowed = if provisional && !runner.context.is_system() {
                    runner.authorize_read(&read.table, &row, prepaid)?
                } else { trusted || runner.check_rule(&read.table, Effect::Read, None, &row)? };
                if !allowed { if !runner.read_capped() {runner.hidden_row();} return Ok(None); }
                // @ref LLP 3000.001#proposed-composition-of-retained-and-authoritative-reads — a delivered left point hit marks only its component, never whole-query coverage or a miss.
                if runner.device && runner.retained_row(&read.table, &row)? { runner.components.note(|frame| frame.retained = true); }
                runner.note_expiry(table, &row);
                let projected = runner.disclosed_row(table, row).for_query();

                let visible = projected.into_value(); // drop hidden storage before leaving the internal scope
                Ok(Some(AuthorizedRow { projection: runner.disclosed_row(table, visible).for_query(), authority: RowAuthorization(()), expiry: Default::default() }))
            })?;
            // The live aggregate is an authorized result computation. It must keep
            // its ordinary work budget and cannot publish a partial scalar value.
            let Some(authorized) = authorized else {
                if !*absent && self.read_capped() { self.point_cut(read, key); }
                return Ok(None);
            };
            expiry.emit();
            authorized.into_value()
        };
        // A maintained point read has already reserved its recount scratch (aggregate_point_scratch).
        let row = if prepaid_examined { self.live_aggregate_prepaid(&read.table, row)? } else { self.live_aggregate(&read.table, row)? }.unwrap_or(Value::Null);
        Ok(Some(AuthorizedRow { projection: self.disclosed_row(table, row).for_query(), authority: RowAuthorization(()), expiry: Default::default() }))
    }
        self.refuse_engine_owned(table, Effect::Insert)?;
        self.refuse_engine_owned(table, Effect::Update)
    }

    /// Programs never write a maintained target; the engine does.
    pub(super) fn refuse_engine_owned(&self, table: &str, effect: Effect) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        // @ref LLP 3000.022#14.3 — pending member changes cannot rewrite settled requirements.
        if self.device && effect != Effect::Insert && self.schema.receipts.iter().any(|f| f.members == table || f.runs == table) {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Prediction("receipt membership changes and progress queue until settlement".into()));
        }
        self.refuse_receipt_table(table)?;
        self.refuse_condition_write()?;
        self.refuse_timeline_write(table, effect)?;
        self.schema.refuse_private(table)?;
        if self.schema.maintain_for(table).is_some() {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Schema(format!("{table} is a maintained aggregate; write its source rows instead")));
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    fn check_constraints(&mut self, table_name: &str, old: Option<&Value>, new: &Value, effect: Effect) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        let table = self.schema.table(table_name).expect("validated");
        let id = new.field("id")?.as_str()?.to_owned();
        // @ref LLP 3000.039#4.1 — an action's own write consults no rule: probing the child's read rule would draw the shared allowance only where a child exists.
        let readable = match old { Some(old) if effect == Effect::Update && !self.engine_action => {

//! A positional unique key followed by named tests is named-key sugar.
// @ref LLP 3000.013#2.2 — only declared unique keys resolve positions; extra equalities test one row.
use super::{catalog::{Catalog, TableInfo}, intent_walk as walk};
use crate::{ast::*, diag::{Diagnostic, Fallible, Site}, print};
use std::{borrow::Cow, collections::BTreeMap};

pub(super) fn keys<'a>(table: &TableInfo, parts: &'a [KeyPart], index: Option<&str>, site: &Site, synthesized: &std::collections::BTreeSet<(String, String)>, types: Option<&[(crate::types::Ty, bool)]>) -> Fallible<Cow<'a, [KeyPart]>> {
    if !parts.iter().any(|key| key.column.is_some()) || !parts.iter().any(|key| key.column.is_none()) { return Ok(Cow::Borrowed(parts)); }
    let prefix = parts.iter().take_while(|key| key.column.is_none()).count();
    if let Some(late) = parts[prefix..].iter().find(|key| key.column.is_none()) {
        let ordered = parts.iter().filter(|key| key.column.is_none()).chain(parts.iter().filter(|key| key.column.is_some())).map(spelling).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");
        return Err(Diagnostic::new(&late.site, "E_QUARRY_KEY_ORDER", "positional key components come before named column tests",
            format!("{}[{ordered}]", table.decl.name)).guide("reads"));
    }
    let declared: Vec<_> = table.indexes.iter().filter(|key| key.unique
        && !synthesized.contains(&(table.decl.name.clone(), key.name.clone()))).collect();
    let candidates: Vec<_> = if let Some(types) = types {
        let read = ReadExpr { table: table.decl.name.clone(), keys: parts[..prefix].to_vec(), index: index.map(str::to_owned), site: site.clone(),
            cursor_prefix: None, computed: Default::default(), order_keys: Vec::new(), range: None, matching: None, filter: None, seeks: Vec::new() };
        super::positional_keys::candidates(table, &read, types, synthesized).into_iter()
            .filter(|key| key.unique && key.columns.len() == prefix).collect()
    } else {
        // Before types are known, do not silently turn `roles[viewer,
        // role = 'staff']` into an id lookup merely because byId has the
        // positional prefix's arity. A declared composite key covered by the
        // positional prefix plus named suffix must reach the typed pass, which
        // prints the fully named repair.
        let named: Vec<&str> = parts[prefix..].iter().filter_map(|part| part.column.as_deref()).collect();
        let partial_composite = declared.iter().any(|key| key.columns.len() > prefix && key.columns.len() <= parts.len()
            && key.columns[prefix..].iter().all(|column| named.contains(&column.as_str()))
            && index.is_none_or(|name| name == key.name));
        if partial_composite { Vec::new() }
        else { declared.iter().copied().filter(|key| key.columns.len() == prefix && index.is_none_or(|name| name == key.name)).collect() }
    };
    let chosen = match candidates.as_slice() {
        [chosen] if table.total_unique(chosen) => *chosen,
        _ => {
            let suggested = candidates.first().copied().or_else(|| declared.iter().copied().find(|key| key.columns.len() > prefix)).or_else(|| declared.first().copied());
            let descriptions = declared.iter().map(|key| format!("({})", key.columns.join(", "))).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" or ");
            let rewrite = if let Some(key) = suggested {
                let mut named = key.columns.iter().enumerate().map(|(at, column)| {
                    let value = parts.get(at).filter(|_| at < prefix).map(|part| print::expression(&part.value))
                        .or_else(|| parts.iter().find(|part| part.column.as_ref() == Some(column)).map(|part| print::expression(&part.value)))
                        .unwrap_or_else(|| format!("<{column}>"));
                    format!("{column} = {value}")
                }).collect::<Vec<_>>();
                named.extend(parts[prefix..].iter().filter(|part| !key.columns.contains(part.column.as_ref().unwrap())).map(spelling));
                format!("{}[{}]", table.decl.name, named.join(", "))
            } else { format!("{}[id = <id>, {}]", table.decl.name, parts[prefix..].iter().map(spelling).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ")) };
            return Err(Diagnostic::new(site, "E_QUARRY_POSITIONAL_SCAN",
                format!("{} needs one full declared unique key with non-null columns before named tests; {prefix} positional components match {} keys; declared keys: {descriptions}", table.decl.name, candidates.len()), rewrite.clone())
                .source_rewrite(crate::diag::SourceRewriteKind::MixedKey, rewrite).guide("reads"));
        }
    };
    let mut named = parts.to_vec();
    for (part, column) in named.iter_mut().zip(&chosen.columns) { part.column = Some(column.clone()); }
    Ok(Cow::Owned(named))
}

fn spelling(part: &KeyPart) -> String {
    match &part.column { Some(column) => format!("{column} = {}", print::expression(&part.value)), None => print::expression(&part.value) }
}

pub(super) fn read<'a>(table: &TableInfo, read: &'a ReadExpr, catalog: &Catalog) -> Fallible<Cow<'a, ReadExpr>> {
    read_typed(table, read, catalog, None)
}

pub(super) fn read_typed<'a>(table: &TableInfo, read: &'a ReadExpr, catalog: &Catalog, types: Option<&[(crate::types::Ty, bool)]>) -> Fallible<Cow<'a, ReadExpr>> {
    Ok(match keys(table, &read.keys, read.index.as_deref(), &read.site, &catalog.synthesized, types)? {
        Cow::Borrowed(_) => Cow::Borrowed(read),
        Cow::Owned(keys) => {
            let index = types.and_then(|types| super::positional_keys::candidates(table, read, types, &catalog.synthesized)
                .first().map(|index| index.name.clone())).or_else(|| read.index.clone());
            Cow::Owned(ReadExpr { keys, index, ..read.clone() })
        },
    })
}

/// Normalize before planning and sync inference, including helpers and shapes.
/// Invalid keys remain for the ordinary binder to diagnose at their use site.
pub(super) fn normalize(catalog: &mut Catalog) {
    let tables: BTreeMap<_, _> = catalog.tables.clone();
    let mut visit = |value: &mut Expr| {
        let (name, parts, index, site) = match value {
            Expr::Read(read) | Expr::Exists { read, .. } | Expr::Created { read, .. } => (&read.table, &mut read.keys, read.index.as_deref(), &read.site),
            Expr::Write { table, keys, site, .. } => (&*table, keys, None, &*site),
            _ => return,
        };
        if let Some(table) = tables.get(name)
            && let Ok(Cow::Owned(named)) = keys(table, parts, index, site, &catalog.synthesized, None) { *parts = named; }
    };
    for table in catalog.tables.values_mut() {
        for rule in &mut table.decl.rules { walk::expression(&mut rule.value, &mut visit); }
        for (value, _) in &mut table.decl.final_exists { walk::expression(value, &mut visit); }
    }
    for doc in &mut catalog.documents {
        walk::document(doc, &mut visit);
        for view in &mut doc.views { for branch in &mut view.branches {
            if let Some(predicate) = &mut branch.predicate { walk::expression(predicate, &mut visit); }
            walk::shape(&mut branch.fields, &mut visit);
        } }
    }
    catalog.shapes = catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|doc| doc.shapes.iter().cloned().map(|shape| (shape.name.clone(), shape))).collect();
    catalog.functions = catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|doc| doc.functions.iter().cloned().map(|function| (function.name.clone(), function))).collect();
}
            }
            ordered.push(keys[position].1.clone());
        }
        Ok(ordered)
    }

    fn walk(&mut self, table: &str, seed: &A, column: &str, depth: u32, site: &Site, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Recurse, Ty)> {
        let info = self.table(table, site)?;
        let Some(next) = info.columns.get(column) else {
            return Err(unknown(site, format!("{table} has no column {column}"), name_rewrite(column, info.columns.keys().map(String::as_str), None)));
        };
        if !matches!(next.kind, ast::TypeKind::Ref { table: ref t } if t == table) {
            return Err(type_error(site, format!("walk follows a reference back to {table}; {table}.{column} is {}", print::scalar_type(next)), format!("{column}: {table} ?")));
        }
        if depth == 0 || depth > 64 {
            return Err(unbound_loop(site, "walk depth is between 1 and 64", format!("walk {table}[seed] up .{column} depth 8")));
        }
        let (seed_expr, _) = self.expr_as_reference(seed, out, &Ty::Ref(table.to_owned()))?;
        Ok((Recurse { site: self.site(), table: table.to_owned(), index: "byId".into(), seed: Box::new(seed_expr), next_field: column.to_owned(), depth, fanout: 1, provenance: KeyProvenance::Computed }, Ty::Row(table.to_owned())))
    }

    fn merge(&mut self, merge: &ast::MergeExpr, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {
        if merge.lanes.iter().any(|lane| lane.planned.is_some()) || merge.index.is_empty() { return self.planned_view(merge, out, prefix::Position::Page); }
        let info = self.table(&merge.table, &merge.site)?;
        if merge.lanes.first().and_then(|lane| lane.table.as_deref()).is_some_and(|table| table != merge.table) {
            return Err(type_error(&merge.site, "the first merge lane must use the header table, which owns the cursor", "start with [prefix] for the header table").guide("feeds"));
        }
        let Some(index) = info.indexes.iter().find(|index| index.name == merge.index).cloned() else {
            return Err(unknown(&merge.site, format!("{} has no index {}", merge.table, merge.index), name_rewrite(&merge.index, info.indexes.iter().map(|i| i.name.as_str()), None)));
        };
        // @ref LLP 3000.007#18.1 — matching merges lower to scoped matching scans in the existing lane IR.
        let direction = if merge.order == ast::Order::Asc { "first" } else { "last" };
        let matching_form = |scope: &[String]| format!("merge {} matching q{} {direction} {} by {}:\n  [{}]", merge.table, if merge.cursor.is_some() { " after c" } else { "" }, merge.take, merge.index, scope.join(", "));
        if let Some((position, _)) = &index.words {
            let scope = &index.columns[..*position];
            if merge.matching.is_none() {
                return Err(unsupported(&merge.site, "merge over a words index without matching", matching_form(scope)).guide("search"));
            }
            if merge.lanes.iter().any(|lane| lane.prefix.len() != *position || lane.columns.as_ref().is_some_and(|columns| columns.iter().flatten().any(|column| !scope.contains(column)))) {
                return Err(type_error(&merge.site, format!("matching merge lanes must pin exactly the {} scope column(s) before words in {}.{}", position, merge.table, merge.index), matching_form(scope)).guide("search"));
            }
        } else if merge.matching.is_some() {
            return Err(unsupported(&merge.site, "matching on a merge over a plain index", format!("merge {} {direction} {} by {}:\n  [prefix]  -- or merge {} matching q {direction} {} by <wordsIndex>:\n  [scope]", merge.table, merge.take, merge.index, merge.table, merge.take)).guide("search"));
        }
        let width = merge.lanes.first().map(|lane| lane.prefix.len()).unwrap_or(0);
        if width >= index.columns.len() {
            return Err(type_error(&merge.site, format!("merge lane prefixes must leave at least one {}.{} suffix column", merge.table, merge.index), "drop the last prefix key from every lane"));
        }
        if merge.lanes.iter().any(|lane| lane.prefix.len() != width) {
            return Err(type_error(&merge.site, "merge lanes must have equal-width index prefixes", format!("use {width} prefix key(s) in every lane")));
        }
        let mut cursor_expr = None;
        if let Some(cursor) = &merge.cursor {
            let (e, t) = self.expr(cursor, out)?;
            if !matches!(t.required(), Ty::Cursor) {
                return Err(type_error(cursor.site(), format!("merge after expects a cursor, got {}", t.name()), "declare c: cursor ? and pass after c"));
            }
            cursor_expr = Some(e);
        }
        let merge_site = self.site();
        let matching = merge.matching.as_ref().map(|value| self.expr(value, out)).transpose()?;
        let matching = matching.map(|(value, ty)| {
                };
                let ir_op = match op {
                    "+" => BinaryOp::Add,
                    "-" => BinaryOp::Sub,
                    _ => BinaryOp::Mul,
                };
                return Ok((Expr::Binary { op: ir_op, left: Box::new(l), right: Box::new(r) }, result));
            }
            other => return Err(unknown(site, format!("unknown operator {other}"), "use =, !=, <, <=, >, >=, +, -, *, and, or, ??")),
        };
        let mut present = Vec::new();
        if matches!(ir_op, BinaryOp::Lt | BinaryOp::Lte | BinaryOp::Gt | BinaryOp::Gte) {
            if nullable_left { present.push(l.clone()); }
            if nullable_right { present.push(r.clone()); }
        }
        let mut comparison = Expr::Binary { op: ir_op, left: Box::new(l), right: Box::new(r) };
        // @ref LLP 3000.013#2.1 — predicate nulls do not enter ordering;
        // the ordinary interpreter's total value ordering remains unchanged.
        for value in present.into_iter().rev() {
            comparison = Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::And,
                left: Box::new(Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::Ne, left: Box::new(value), right: Box::new(ir::lit(Value::Null)) }),
                right: Box::new(comparison) };
        }
        Ok((comparison, Ty::Bool))
    }

    /// An expression in an identity position: a text literal becomes an id.
    pub(super) fn expr_as_identity(&mut self, expr: &A, out: &mut setup::Body, expected: &Ty) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {
        if let A::Literal { value: Literal::Str(text), .. } = expr {
            if text.is_empty() {
                return Err(type_error(expr.site(), "an empty text literal cannot be an id", "use a non-empty id literal, or null"));
            }
            return Ok((Expr::Literal(Value::Id(text.clone())), expected.required()));
        }
        self.expr(expr, out)
    }

    pub(super) fn expr_as_reference(&mut self, expr: &A, out: &mut setup::Body, expected: &Ty) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {
        let (value, ty) = self.expr_as_identity(expr, out, expected)?;
        Ok(self.row_identity(value, ty, expected))
    }

    // @ref LLP 3000.036.002#8 — contextual projection is exactly `.id`,
    // without a lookup; optional and cross-table rows still need an edit.
    pub(super) fn row_identity(&self, value: Expr, ty: Ty, expected: &Ty) -> (Expr, Ty) {
        if !ty.is_optional() && matches!(ty.required(), Ty::Row(_) | Ty::WriteRow(_)) {
            let table = match ty.required() { Ty::Row(t) | Ty::WriteRow(t) => t, _ => unreachable!() };
            if expected.required() == Ty::Ref(table.clone()) {
                return (ir::field(value, "id"), Ty::Ref(table));
            }
        }
        (value, ty)
    }

    pub(super) fn call(&mut self, name: &str, args: &[A], site: &Site, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {
        if let Some(result) = self.job_query_call(name, args, site, out) { return result; }

                let (expr, ty) = self.expr(other, out)?;
                match ty {
                    Ty::List(item, max) => Ok((LoopDomain::DerivedArray { value: expr, max, bound_source: "list".into() }, *item)),
                    Ty::Page(table, max) => Ok((LoopDomain::DerivedArray { value: expr, max, bound_source: "page".into() }, Ty::Row(table))),
                    Ty::Collection(item, max) => Ok((LoopDomain::DerivedArray { value: expr, max, bound_source: "shape".into() }, *item)),
                    Ty::Optional(inner) if inner.loop_max().is_some() => Err(type_error(other.site(), "for cannot iterate an optional list", format!("for x in {} ?? []:", print::expression(other)))),
                    Ty::Unknown => Err(unbound_loop(other.site(), "for cannot prove a static bound for this value", "iterate a bounded page, a bounded list argument, or tokens(text)")),
                    other_ty => Err(type_error(other.site(), format!("for cannot iterate {}", other_ty.name()), "iterate a bounded page or bounded list")),
                }
            }
        }
    }

    /// Arguments belong to the caller: bind and snapshot each one before
    /// introducing any parameter. `new` and reads are evaluated once, in
    /// source order, even when the helper uses the parameter repeatedly.
    fn helper_arguments(&mut self, name: &str, declared: &[ast::ArgDecl], args: &[A], site: &Site, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<BTreeMap<String, Binding>> {
        if self.depth >= MAX_EXPANSION_DEPTH {
            return Err(unsupported(site, "helpers nested deeper than eight levels", "flatten the helper"));
        }
        if args.len() != declared.len() {
            return Err(type_error(site, format!("{name} takes {} arguments; received {}", declared.len(), args.len()), format!("{name}({})", declared.iter().map(|a| a.name.clone()).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", "))));
        }
        let mut bindings = BTreeMap::new();
        for (declared, given) in declared.iter().zip(args) {
            if bindings.contains_key(&declared.name) {
                return Err(unknown(&declared.site, format!("duplicate argument {}", declared.name), "rename one helper parameter"));
            }
            let expected = Ty::from_scalar(&declared.type_);
            // Helpers are polymorphic over same-table row snapshots; retain their
            // existing row parameter semantics. Only a literal needs id conversion.
            let (expr, ty) = if expected.required().is_identity() && matches!(given, A::Literal { .. }) { self.expr_as_identity(given, out, &expected)? } else { self.expr(given, out)? };
            // @ref LLP 3000.036.002#8 — call-site expansion preserves a same-table row; references keep their existing meaning.
            let expected = match (expected.required(), ty.required()) {
                (Ty::Ref(table), Ty::Row(row)) if table == row && (!ty.is_optional() || expected.is_optional()) => {
                    if expected.is_optional() { Ty::Row(row).optional() } else { Ty::Row(row) }
                }
                (Ty::Ref(table), Ty::WriteRow(row)) if table == row && (!ty.is_optional() || expected.is_optional()) => {
                    if expected.is_optional() { Ty::WriteRow(row).optional() } else { Ty::WriteRow(row) }
                }
                _ => expected,
            };
            if !ty.assignable_to(&expected) {
                return Err(type_error(given.site(), format!("{name}'s {} is {}; got {}", declared.name, expected.name(), ty.name()), match (ty.required(), expected.required()) {
                    (Ty::Enum(variants), Ty::Text(max)) if variants.iter().any(|variant| variant.chars().count() > max as usize) => format!("change {name}'s parameter to `{}: {}`; not every variant fits text <={max}", declared.name, ty.name()),
                    _ if ty.is_optional() && !expected.is_optional() => self.parent_rewrite(given).unwrap_or_else(|| format!("{} — require this argument once before calling {name}; absent or hidden rows refuse NOT_FOUND", print::expression(&A::Assert { value: Box::new(given.clone()), code: "NOT_FOUND".into(), message: None, site: given.site().clone() }))),
                    _ => format!("pass {} for {}; a table parameter receives an id when called with a reference, or the same-table row snapshot when called with a row. To read fields from an id, bind `{table}[{}] ! else NOT_FOUND` in the helper; multiline fn bodies need `return value`", expected.name(), declared.name, declared.name, table = match expected.required() { Ty::Ref(t) => t, _ => "<table>".into() }),
                }));
            }
            let runtime = self.temp(&declared.name);
            self.maintenance_scopes.insert(runtime.clone(), format!("helper/{name}/parameter/{}", declared.name));
            out.push(Stmt::Let { name: runtime.clone(), value: expr });
            bindings.insert(declared.name.clone(), Binding { runtime, ty: expected.clone(), declared: expected, parameter: true, revision: 0, fields: BTreeMap::new(), error: None, origins: Vec::new() });
        }
        Ok(bindings)
    }

    pub(super) fn fn_call(&mut self, function: &ast::FunctionDecl, args: &[A], site: &Site, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {
        let bindings = self.helper_arguments(&function.name, &function.args, args, site, out)?;
        let start = out.len();
        let caller = std::mem::replace(&mut self.scopes, vec![bindings]);
        self.declare(pure_calls::CONSTANTS, Ty::Null);

//! A row-read verdict and field projection together authorize result pricing.
use super::runner::Runner;
use crate::{error::{MeterKind, RuntimeError}, ir::Read, meter::Meter,
    schema::{Effect, Index}, value::{Disclosed, Value}};

// Only successful row authorization in this module can mint this evidence.
pub(crate) struct RowAuthorization(());
pub(crate) struct AuthorizedRow<'a> {
    projection: Disclosed<'a>,
    authority: RowAuthorization,
    // @ref LLP 3000.014#5 — a row's witness deadline is provisional until an answer emits it.
    expiry: super::expiry::Pending,
}
impl AuthorizedRow<'_> {
    pub(super) fn expiry(&self) -> super::expiry::Pending { self.expiry }
    pub(super) fn scalar_heap(&self, field: &str) -> u64 { self.projection.scalar_heap(field, &self.authority) }
    pub(crate) fn heap_bytes(&self) -> u64 { self.projection.heap_bytes(&self.authority) }
    pub(super) fn projection_heap(&self) -> u64 { self.projection.projection_heap(&self.authority) }
    pub(crate) fn materialization_heap(&self) -> u64 { self.projection.materialization_heap(&self.authority) }
    pub(super) fn into_value(self) -> Value { self.projection.into_value() }
    pub(super) fn into_parts(self) -> (Value, RowAuthorization) { (self.projection.into_value(), self.authority) }
    pub(super) fn charge(self, meter: &mut Meter) -> Result<Value, RuntimeError> {
        meter.heap(self.heap_bytes() + self.projection_heap() + self.materialization_heap())?;
        Ok(self.into_value())
    }
}

impl RowAuthorization {
    pub(super) fn project(self, projection: Disclosed<'_>) -> AuthorizedRow<'_> { AuthorizedRow { projection, authority:self, expiry: Default::default() } }
}

impl<'r> Runner<'r> {
    // @ref LLP 3000.015#1 — member reads share the row-read and field-disclosure capability.
    pub(super) fn authorized_member(&mut self, name: &str, id: &str, #[cfg(feature = "server-search")] trusted: bool) -> Result<Option<Value>, RuntimeError> {
        let table = &self.schema.tables[name];
        // @ref LLP 3000.024#decision — this id came from an authorized head in
        // this read-only transaction. Price its public image before decoding,
        // then recheck the rule with a separate witness reservation. Neither
        // hidden/expired images nor private fields can select actual-size admission.
        #[cfg(feature = "server-search")]
        if trusted && !self.device && !table.read_lifetime_sensitive() && table.recorded.is_empty()
            && table.through.is_none() && table.written_by.values().all(|a|a.audience) {
            if !self.read_charge(MeterKind::Examined,1)? { return Ok(None); }
            self.meter.heap(crate::rows::word_heap::text(id) + crate::rows::word_heap::slots::<Value>(1))?;
            let key = [Value::Id(id.into())];
            let Some((Some(row),_)) = self.scan_authorized(name,crate::schema::BY_ID,&key,&Default::default(),crate::rows::Dir::Asc,1,true,true,None)?.pop() else { return Ok(None); };
            let materialization = row.materialization_heap();
            let prepay = self.meter.mark();
            self.meter.heap(materialization)?;
            let expiry = self.next_expiry.scope();
            let row = self.authorized_scratch(self.rule_scratch(&table.rules.read), &[(MeterKind::RuleProbes,super::rules::rule_probes(&table.rules.read),
                )], |runner,_| {
                let row = runner.authorize_image(name,row.into_value())?;
                if let Some(row) = &row { runner.next_expiry.inherit(row.expiry()); }
                Ok(row)
            });
            // The value now owns the default. Transfer its prepay to charge(),
            // keeping only one retained reservation after the recheck.
            self.meter.release_row_heap(prepay, materialization);
            let row = row?;
            if row.is_some() { expiry.emit(); }
            return row.map(|row|row.charge(&mut self.meter)).transpose();
        }
        let bytes = crate::rows::disclosed::decode_reservation(table).saturating_add(self.rule_scratch(&table.rules.read));
        let expiry = self.next_expiry.scope();
        let row = self.authorized_scratch(bytes, &[(MeterKind::Examined, 1), (MeterKind::RuleProbes, super::rules::rule_probes(&table.rules.read),
                )], |runner, scope| {
            let Some(mut row) = runner.tx.get(scope, name, id)? else { return Ok(None); };
            crate::schema::materialize_optional_fields(&mut row, table);
            let until = table.deadline(&row);
            let authorized = runner.authorize_image(name, row)?;
            if let Some(row) = &authorized { runner.next_expiry.inherit(row.expiry()); runner.next_expiry.note_option(until); }
            Ok(authorized)
        })?;
        if row.is_some() { expiry.emit(); }
        row.map(|row|row.charge(&mut self.meter)).transpose()
    }

    pub(super) fn authorize_image(&mut self, name: &str, row: Value) -> Result<Option<AuthorizedRow<'r>>, RuntimeError> {
        let table = &self.schema.tables[name];
        // @ref LLP 3000.014#5-what-derives-from-the-capture — a denied or expired candidate's witness deadlines are discarded with its scope.
        let expiry = self.next_expiry.scope();
        if self.inactive(name, &row)? { return Ok(None); }
        if !self.check_rule(name, Effect::Read, None, &row)? {
            self.hidden_row();
            return Ok(None);
        }
        // Memory deadlines are hidden by Disclosed itself; transfer the live
        // boundary before projecting, still inside this candidate's expiry scope.
        if table.ephemeral.is_some() { self.note_expiry(table, &row); }
        let projection = self.disclosed_row(table, row).for_query();
        let visible = projection.into_value();
        Ok(Some(AuthorizedRow { projection: self.disclosed_row(table, visible).for_query(), authority: RowAuthorization(()), expiry: expiry.defer() }))
    }

    pub(super) fn scan_disclosed(&mut self, name: &str, index: &str, prefix: &[Value], bounds: &crate::rows::Bounds, dir: crate::rows::Dir, limit: usize) -> Result<Vec<(Option<AuthorizedRow<'r>>, Vec<u8>)>, RuntimeError> {
        self.scan_authorized(name,index,prefix,bounds,dir,limit,true,true,#[cfg(feature = "server-search")] None)
    }

    pub(super) fn scan_authorized(&mut self, name: &str, index: &str, prefix: &[Value], bounds: &crate::rows::Bounds, dir: crate::rows::Dir, limit: usize, metered: bool, trusted: bool, #[cfg(feature = "server-search")] fields: Option<&[String]>) -> Result<Vec<(Option<AuthorizedRow<'r>>, Vec<u8>)>, RuntimeError> {

    /// A system read of an expiring table takes the store path, so the store
    /// would price every fetched entry. Open the authored range once, step
    /// over expired entries internally, and price live entries through the
    /// same canonical-image codec used by every store.
    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.24.6 — the public meter charges only rows live at the read's clock.
    #[cfg_attr(not(feature = "sqlite"), allow(dead_code))]
    fn live_store_scan(&mut self, name: &str, index: &str, prefix: &[Value], bounds: &crate::rows::Bounds, dir: crate::rows::Dir, limit: usize, metered: bool) -> Result<Vec<(Value, Vec<u8>)>, RuntimeError> {
        let mut cursor = bounds.clone();
        let mut out = Vec::new();
        // Price the authored range once. Internal cursors over expired keys
        // cannot select another public scan-opening charge or wider bounds.
        let mut guard = super::authorized_scan::InternalLedger::with_meter(&mut self.meter, |meter, scope| {
            if metered { crate::rows::word_heap::scan_open(meter, name, index, prefix, bounds, scope)?; }
            Ok::<_, RuntimeError>(crate::rows::word_heap::StoreGuard::open(meter, super::read::EXPIRED_STEP_GUARD))
        })?;
        while out.len() < limit {
            let window = self.metered_scan(|runner, scope| {
                runner.tx.scan(scope,name,index,prefix,&cursor,dir,limit - out.len())
            })?;
            if window.is_empty() { break; }
            for (row, key) in window {
                cursor.after = crate::key::decode(&key).map(|v|v.into_iter().skip(prefix.len()).collect());
                if self.step_inactive(name, &row, &key)? { continue; }
                if metered {
                    let additional = super::authorized_scan::InternalLedger::with_meter(&mut self.meter, |meter, _| {
                        crate::rows::word_heap::scan_value_entry(meter, &mut guard, out.len(), out.capacity(), &row, &key)
                    })?;
                    if additional != 0 { out.reserve_exact(additional); }
                }
                out.push((row, key));
            }
        }
        Ok(out)
    }

    /// One fetched candidate's expiry, rule and field verdict, inside an
    /// internal scope. Unadmitted rule work is an unknown fact on a device.
    fn candidate_verdict(&mut self, scope: &super::authorized_scan::Authorization, name: &str, index: &str, prefix: &[Value], mut row: Value, trusted: bool, rule_prepaid: bool) -> Result<Option<AuthorizedRow<'r>>, RuntimeError> {
        let table = &self.schema.tables[name];
        crate::schema::materialize_optional_fields(&mut row,table);
        let provisional = self.is_provisional(name,&row)?;
        let needs_rule = !self.context.is_system() && (!trusted || provisional);
        if needs_rule && !rule_prepaid && !self.device_work_active && !scope.admit_probes(super::rules::rule_probes(&table.rules.read)) {
            // @ref LLP 3000.020#3.2 — unresolved authority is unknown, not an absent contribution.
            if self.device { self.unknown_fact(name, index, prefix, "source rule admission"); }
            return Ok(None);
        }
        if trusted && !provisional && self.schema.maintain_for(name).is_some_and(|m| m.parent_key.is_some()) {
            return Ok(Some(self.authorized_synthetic(table, row)));
        }
        self.authorize_image(name,row)
    }

    /// Cold certification has already admitted the member and entire source
    /// group, including a decoder page reserved before entering prepaid work. Every row
    /// still receives the ordinary expiry, rule and field verdict below.
    // @ref LLP 3000.015#3 — live count certification shares its transaction and authority.
    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
    pub(super) fn scan_acquisition_basis(&mut self, name: &str, index: &str, prefix: &[Value], bounds: &crate::rows::Bounds) -> Result<Vec<(Option<AuthorizedRow<'r>>, Vec<u8>)>, RuntimeError> {
